Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the regular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 505. The moduli space has dimension at most 637 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_505(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 505. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^637(S, F) is trivial. QED.
